Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than
Ac. 2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar
Mandal, Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the
Government as care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee
Wo. Burhanuddin, and in view of the land made into small plots to an extent of
100 Sq. Yards to each petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs
of Smt. Haneefa Bee viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma
by way of Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation
dl.2O-O3-2O23 followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust
unconstitutional and violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia
and consequently direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and
to deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the
petitioners as per their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated
in the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. 2O-O3-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition,
in the interest of justice.
Counsel for the Petitioners: Mr. SHAIK JILANI
Counsel for the Respondent No.1: AGP FOR GENERAL ADMINISTRATION
Counsel for the Respondent No.2, 7, 8 & 10 to 15: GP FOR REVENUE
Counsel for the Respondent No.3 & 6: GP FOR FOREST
Counsel for the Respondent No.4: GP FOR MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION AND
URBAN DEVELOPMENT
Counsel for the Respondent No.5, I & 16 to 18: GP FOR HOME

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 06-04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, uniust unconstitutional and violative
of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. 06-04-2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition,
in the interest of justice.
Counsel for the Petitioners: Mr. SHAIK JILANI
Counsel for the Respondent No.1: AGP FOR GENERAL ADMINISTRATION
Counsel for the Respondent No.2, 7, I & 10 to 15: GP FOR REVENUE
Counsel for the Respondent No.3 & 6: GP FOR FOREST
Counsel for the Respondent No.4: GP FOR MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION AND
URBAN OEVELOPMENT
Counsel for the Respondent No.S, 9 & 16 to 18: GP FOR HOME

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in

at') 65
l0
the nature of Writ of fvlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than
Ac. 2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar
Mandal, Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the
Government as care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo.
Burhanuddin, and in view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq.
yards to each petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt.
Haneefa Bee viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way
of Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 27.03.2023
followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and
violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia and consequently
direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the
vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per
their Memorandum of Gift.
ANO:1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. 27.O3.2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in
the interest of lustice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of lri'landamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than
Ac. 2400.00 gts,, in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar
Mandal, Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the
Government as care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee
W/o. Burhanuddin, and in view of the land made into small plots to an extent of
100 Sq. Yards to each petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs
of Smt. Haneefa Bee viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma
by way of Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 04-04-
2023 followed by personal visits, as being illegat, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional
and violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and
consequently direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and to
deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners
as per their Memorandum of Gift, and to grant such other relief or reliefs as this
Hon'ble Court deems fit and proper in the circumstances of the case and in the
interest of justice.
A NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. 04.O4.2023, pending disposal of the main Writ petition, in
the interest of justice-Counsel for the Petitioners: SRI SHAIK JILANI

Counsel for the Respondent No.2, 7, 8 & 10 to 15: GP FOR REVENUE
CounseJfor the Respondent No.3 & 6: GP FOR FOREST

URBAN DEVELOPMENT

Petition under Article 226 of lhe constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee w/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt' Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 03-04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative
of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of India and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peacefulandphysicalpossessionoftheplotstothepetitionersaspertheir
Memorandum of Gift-IANO:1OF 2024

the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. o3-o4-2}23, pending disposal of the main writ Petition'

authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than

Mandal, Ranga
. Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the

Respondents
Petition under Article 226 of lhe Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than
Ac.2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar
Mandal, Range Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the
Government as care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee
Wo. Burhanuddin, and in view of the land made into small plots to an extent of
'1O0 Sq. Yards to each petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs
of Smt. Haneefa Bee viz., ltld. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma
by way of Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt.
2O-O7 -2023 followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust
unconstitutional and violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia
and consequently direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and
to deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the
petitioners as per their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. 2O-O7 -2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition,
in the interest of justice.
Counsel for the Petitioners: Mr. SHAIK JILANI

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than
Ac.2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar
Mandal, Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the
Government as care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o.
Burhanuddin, and in view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq.
Yards to each petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt.
Haneefa Bee viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way
of Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 08-04-2023
followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and
violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently
direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the
vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per
their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
direct the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant
to the representation dt. o8-O4-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition'
in the interest of justice.
Counsel for the Petitioner: Mr. SHAIK JILANI
Counsel for the Respondent No.1: AGP FOR GENERAL ADMINISTRATION
Counsel for the Respondent No.2, 7, 8 & 10 to 15: GP FOR REVENUE
Counsel for the Respondent No.3 & 6: GP FOR FOREST
Counsel for the Respondent No.4: GP FOR MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION AND
URBAN DEVELOPMENT
Counsel for the Respondent No.S, 9 & 16 to 18: GP FOR HOME

HONOURABLE SRI JUSTICE B. VTJAYSEN REDDY

2024
COMMON ORDER:
Leamed counsel for the petitioners submits that subject
matter of these writ petitions is squarely covered by the common
order passed by this Court in W.P.No.220 of 2024 and batch, dated
05.01.2024, and the same is not disputed by the leamed Assistant
Govemment Pleader appearing for the respondents.
2. Therefore, recording aforesaid submissions of the learned
counsel, [ollowing the common order dated 05.01.2024 passed by
thls Court in W.P.No.220 of 2024 and batch, in terms thereofl, these
writ petitions are dismissed. However, it is always open to the
petitioners to approach the concerned authorities for allotment of2
BHK houses and avail welfare schemes introduced by the
Government. The said common order shall be made part of the
record and a copy of the same shatl be annexed to this order. No
order as to costs.

Since common issue is involved in all these writ petitions,
with the consent of the both sides, they are being disposed of by
this common order.
2. W.P. No.220 of 2024 is taken as a lead case for disposal of
all these writ petitions.
3. W.P.No.220 of 2024 is filed questioning the action of
respondent authorities in not taking steps to release the land
admeasuring Acs.2400-00 guntas in Survey No.7 situated at
Mansoorabad Viltage, Saroornagar Mandal, Ranga Reddy District,
to the petitioners, pursuant to the written representation of the
petitioners dated 20.03.2023 as being illegal, arbitrary and vlolative
of Articles 21 and 300A of Constitution of India and consequently
direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and
deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of plots to the
petitioners.

16. Learned counsel for the petitioners also relied on
memorandum of gift deed (Ex.P25). It is clear from the contents of
the gift deed that donors Mr. Yousuf Khan and his wife Mrs
Vasam Tulsamma alias Sultana were never ln possession of the
subject land. In fact, the clause 3 and 4 therein clearly state that
the First Party/Donors shall deliver the possession of the said
property to the Second PartylDonee as and when the cases are
completed (dispute regarding the title is resolved and there is also
reference to the W.P. Nos.29928 and 10100 of 2Ol4 which are
pending before this Court) and the First PartylDonors have no
responsibility of the subject land. The Second Pa(y/Donee has
only responsibility of taking the possession of the land

17. ln the opinion of this Court' the petitioners lack foundational
claim. Even according to the petitioners their predecessors were
not in possession of the subject land for the last seventy (70) years
and that since 1947, the subject tand had been in possession of the
Government' It needs to be mentioned that the Andhra Pradesh
Land Reforms (Ceiling on Agricultural Holdings) Act' 1973 (for
short'the Act, 1973') came into force with effect from 01'01'1975'
The persons who hold the land beyond the ceiling limit were
required to file declaration under the provisions of the Act' 1973'
It is stated that Mrs' Haneefa Bee was in possession of the subject
landadmeasuringAcs.2400.00guntas.Primafacie,theholdirrgof
Acs.2400-00 guntas of land is beyond the ceiling limit and contrary
to the provisions of the Act' 1973' As pointed out by learned
Assistant Government Pleaders neither Mrs' Haneefa Bee nor her
successors have filed any apptication for restoration of land' It is
not stated under which law the subject land has been taken into
Government custody. The pleadings of the petitioners did not
throw any tight on that subject' It is vaguety contended by the
learned counsel for the petitioners that the custody of land was
taken under the Atiyat Enquiry Act' It is not stated by the

would not convey any title' Moreover' the attempt made bv the
petitioners as to why the original Smt. Haneefa Bee did not claim
release of lands. There is no clarity as to who ([re appropriate
statutory authority for releasing the lands and it is highly doubtful
if the claim for release can be entertained after more than seventy
It is contended by learned Assistant Government pleader that

reliance is a fake and fabricated document, It is not that only

11
petitioners and others to seek relief in this Court in the earlier

20. Learned counsel for the petitioners also referred to the
judgment of the Apex Court in Civil Appeal No.5779 of 2021
(arising out of SLP (C) No.5730 of 2021). The facts of the said
case do not have any bearing on the dispute involved in this writ
petition.
21. In view of the same, petitioners do not have any locus standi
to institute these writ petitions. There is no merit in the writ
petitions. Accordingly, the writ petitions are dismissed. However,
it is always open to the petitioners to approach the concerned
authorities for allotment of 2 BHK houses and avail welfare
schemes introduced by the Government. There shall be no order as
to costs.
